In our experiments, we empirically evaluate the contextual policies regarding sample-efficiency, zero-shot generalisation to unseen tasks, and robustness to varying water currents. By utilising multi-task reinforcement learning, we aim to improve the training effectiveness, as well as the reusability of learnt policies to take a step towards more sustainable procedures in autonomous reef monitoring.

</details>


### [110] [FeaXDrive: Feasibility-aware Trajectory-Centric Diffusion Planning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12656)
*Baoyun Wang,Zhuoren Li,Ming Liu,Xinrui Zhang,Bo Leng,Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FeaXDrive：一种面向端到端自动驾驶的可行性感知轨迹中心扩散规划方法，通过轨迹中心化建模、自适应曲率约束训练、可行驶区域引导和可行性感知GRPO后训练，显著提升生成轨迹的物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端扩散规划方法生成的轨迹存在物理可行性不足的问题，包括局部几何不规则、违反轨迹级运动学约束、偏离可行驶区域等。这表明当前基于噪声中心的扩散规划公式与轨迹空间的可行性特征不够匹配。

Method: 提出FeaXDrive方法，核心是将干净轨迹作为扩散过程中可行性感知建模的统一对象。基于轨迹中心化公式，集成：1）自适应曲率约束训练以提升内在几何和运动学可行性；2）反向扩散采样中的可行驶区域引导以增强与可行驶区域的一致性；3）可行性感知GRPO后训练以在平衡轨迹空间可行性的同时进一步提升规划性能。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准测试中，FeaXDrive实现了强大的闭环规划性能，同时显著提升了轨迹空间可行性。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在端到端扩散规划中显式建模轨迹空间可行性的重要性，为开发更可靠、物理基础更强的自动驾驶规划器迈出了一步。

Abstract: End-to-end diffusion planning has shown strong potential for autonomous driving, but the physical feasibility of generated trajectories remains insufficiently addressed. In particular, generated trajectories may exhibit local geometric irregularities, violate trajectory-level kinematic constraints, or deviate from the drivable area, indicating that the commonly used noise-centric formulation in diffusion planning is not yet well aligned with the trajectory space where feasibility is more naturally characterized. To address this issue, we propose FeaXDrive, a feasibility-aware trajectory-centric diffusion planning method for end-to-end autonomous driving. The core idea is to treat the clean trajectory as the unified object for feasibility-aware modeling throughout the diffusion process. Built on this trajectory-centric formulation, FeaXDrive integrates adaptive curvature-constrained training to improve intrinsic geometric and kinematic feasibility, drivable-area guidance within reverse diffusion sampling to enhance consistency with the drivable area, and feasibility-aware GRPO post-training to further improve planning performance while balancing trajectory-space feasibility. Experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark show that FeaXDrive achieves strong closed-loop planning performance while substantially improving trajectory-space feasibility. These findings highlight the importance of explicitly modeling trajectory-space feasibility in end-to-end diffusion planning and provide a step toward more reliable and physically grounded autonomous driving planners.

</details>


### [111] [Reliability-Guided Depth Fusion for Glare-Resilient Navigation Costmaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12753)
*Shang-En Tsai,Wei-Cheng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度可靠性建模的抗眩光代价地图构建方法，通过预测像素级测量可信度来减少反射表面导致的虚假障碍物


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决反射性地板和玻璃表面产生的镜面眩光对RGB-D深度测量的干扰问题，这种干扰会导致深度测量产生空洞和尖峰，在占据栅格代价地图中积累为持久的虚假障碍物

Method: 提出基于显式深度可靠性建模的眩光弹性代价地图构建方法：1）轻量级深度可靠性地图（DRM）估计器预测镜面干扰下的像素级测量可信度；2）可靠性引导融合（RGF）机制利用该信号在测量积累到地图前调节占据更新

Result: 在配备Intel RealSense D435和Jetson Orin Nano的真实移动机器人平台上实验表明，该方法在真实反射性地板和玻璃表面条件下显著减少了虚假障碍物插入，改善了自由空间保持，同时仅引入适度的计算开销

Conclusion: 将眩光视为测量可靠性问题，为改善安全关键室内环境中代价地图的正确性和导航鲁棒性提供了一种实用且轻量级的解决方案

Abstract: Specular glare on reflective floors and glass surfaces frequently corrupts RGB-D depth measurements, producing holes and spikes that accumulate as persistent phantom obstacles in occupancy-grid costmaps. This paper proposes a glare-resilient costmap construction method based on explicit depth-reliability modeling. A lightweight Depth Reliability Map (DRM) estimator predicts per-pixel measurement trustworthiness under specular interference, and a Reliability-Guided Fusion (RGF) mechanism uses this signal to modulate occupancy updates before corrupted measurements are accumulated into the map. Experiments on a real mobile robotic platform equipped with an Intel RealSense D435 and a Jetson Orin Nano show that the proposed method substantially reduces false obstacle insertion and improves free-space preservation under real reflective-floor and glass-surface conditions, while introducing only modest computational overhead. These results indicate that treating glare as a measurement-reliability problem provides a practical and lightweight solution for improving costmap correctness and navigation robustness in safety-critical indoor environments.

</details>


### [112] [Actuation space reduction to facilitate insightful shape matching in a novel reconfigurable tendon driven continuum manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12792)
*Sabyasachi Dash,John Golden,Girish Krishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种肌腱驱动连续体机械臂的主动可重构设计，通过旋转间隔盘改变肌腱路径，实现定制化空间变形，并基于曲率-扭率空间分析提出简化的顺序形状匹配控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统肌腱驱动连续体机械臂（TDCMs）的肌腱路径固定，限制了其变形能力。通过主动重新配置肌腱路径，可以实现更灵活的空间变形，但这也增加了从期望形状到驱动器输入的映射复杂性。需要一种简化的控制方法来处理这种可重构系统的复杂性。

Method: 1. 设计主动可重构TDCM：通过旋转单个间隔盘来重新配置肌腱路径，可在驱动前或驱动后进行；2. 将骨干形状投影到曲率-扭率（C-T）空间进行分析，识别对全局形状影响最大的关键盘；3. 提出顺序形状匹配策略：先旋转近端和中间盘近似全局形状，再调整远端盘微调末端执行器位置，对整体形状影响最小。

Result: 在曲率-扭率空间中观察到模式，揭示了哪些盘对实现全局形状最具影响力。基于这一洞察，提出的顺序形状匹配策略能够有效控制可重构TDCM，无需复杂建模。

Conclusion: 提出的驱动框架为可重构肌腱驱动连续体机械臂提供了一种无模型的控制替代方案，避免了传统控制方法中复杂的建模需求，通过简化的顺序形状匹配策略实现了有效的形状控制。

Abstract: In tendon driven continuum manipulators (TDCMs), reconfiguring the tendon routing enables tailored spatial deformation of the backbone. This work presents a design in which tendons can be rerouted either prior to or after actuation by actively rotating the individual spacer disks. Each disk rotation thus adds a degree of freedom to the actuation space, complicating the mapping from a desired backbone curve to the corresponding actuator inputs. However, when the backbone shape is projected into an intermediate space defined by curvature and torsion (C-T), patterns emerge that highlight which disks are most influential in achieving a global shape. This insight enables a simplified, sequential shape-matching strategy: first, the proximal and intermediate disks are rotated to approximate the global shape; then, the distal disks are adjusted to fine-tune the end-effector position with minimal impact on the overall shape. The proposed actuation framework offers a model-free alternative to conventional control approaches, bypassing the complexities of modeling reconfigurable TDCMs.

</details>


### [113] [VULCAN: Vision-Language-Model Enhanced Multi-Agent Cooperative Navigation for Indoor Fire-Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12831)
*Shengding Liu,Qiben Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VULCAN：基于多模态感知和视觉语言模型的多智能体协同导航框架，专为室内火灾救援设计，在模拟火灾环境中评估现有方法并揭示其关键失效模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾灾害给自主搜救带来严峻挑战（浓烟、高温、动态环境），现有多智能体导航系统主要基于视觉且为良性室内环境设计，在火灾动态条件下性能显著下降

Method: 提出VULCAN框架，基于多模态感知和视觉语言模型；扩展Habitat-Matterport3D基准，模拟物理真实的火灾场景（烟雾扩散、热危害、传感器退化）；评估代表性多智能体协同导航基线方法

Result: 揭示了现有方法在火灾场景中的关键失效模式，强调了鲁棒感知和危害感知规划对于可靠多智能体搜救的必要性

Conclusion: 需要开发专门针对火灾灾害响应的多智能体协同导航系统，VULCAN框架展示了多模态感知和视觉语言模型在提升火灾环境下导航鲁棒性方面的潜力

Abstract: Indoor fire disasters pose severe challenges to autonomous search and rescue due to dense smoke, high temperatures, and dynamically evolving indoor environments. In such time-critical scenarios, multi-agent cooperative navigation is particularly useful, as it enables faster and broader exploration than single-agent approaches. However, existing multi-agent navigation systems are primarily vision-based and designed for benign indoor settings, leading to significant performance degradation under fire-driven dynamic conditions. In this paper, we present VULCAN, a multi-agent cooperative navigation framework based on multi-modal perception and vision-language models (VLMs), tailored for indoor fire disaster response. We extend the Habitat-Matterport3D benchmark by simulating physically realistic fire scenarios, including smoke diffusion, thermal hazards, and sensor degradation. We evaluate representative multi-agent cooperative navigation baselines under both normal and fire-driven environments. Our results reveal critical failure modes of existing methods in fire scenarios and underscore the necessity of robust perception and hazard-aware planning for reliable multi-agent search and rescue.

</details>


### [114] [GGD-SLAM: Monocular 3DGS SLAM Powered by Generalizable Motion Model for Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12837)
*Yi Liu,Haoxuan Xu,Hongbo Duan,Keyu Fan,Zhengyang Zhang,Peiyu Zhuang,Pengting Luo,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GGD-SLAM是一个基于3D高斯泼溅表示的视觉SLAM框架，专门针对动态环境设计，无需预定义语义标注或深度输入，通过通用运动模型处理动态场景的定位和稠密建图问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D高斯泼溅表示的视觉SLAM算法在静态环境中表现优异，但在动态环境中性能显著下降，因为它们依赖于静态环境假设。动态环境中的运动物体对定位和建图造成干扰，需要专门的方法来处理。

Method: 1. 使用FIFO队列管理输入帧，通过序列注意力机制提取动态语义特征；2. 集成动态特征增强器分离静态和动态组件；3. 通过静态信息采样填充被遮挡区域；4. 设计针对动态环境的干扰自适应SSIM损失函数，增强系统鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实世界动态数据集上的实验表明，GGD-SLAM在动态场景中的相机姿态估计和稠密重建方面达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: GGD-SLAM通过通用运动模型和专门设计的动态处理机制，成功解决了动态环境中视觉SLAM的挑战，实现了无需语义标注或深度输入的鲁棒定位和建图。

Abstract: Visual SLAM algorithms achieve significant improvements through the exploration of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) representations, particularly in generating high-fidelity dense maps. However, they depend on a static environment assumption and experience significant performance degradation in dynamic environments. This paper presents GGD-SLAM, a framework that employs a generalizable motion model to address the challenges of localization and dense mapping in dynamic environments - without predefined semantic annotations or depth input. Specifically, the proposed system employs a First-In-First-Out (FIFO) queue to manage incoming frames, facilitating dynamic semantic feature extraction through a sequential attention mechanism. This is integrated with a dynamic feature enhancer to separate static and dynamic components. Additionally, to minimize dynamic distractors' impact on the static components, we devise a method to fill occluded areas via static information sampling and design a distractor-adaptive Structure Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) loss tailored for dynamic environments, significantly enhancing the system's resilience. Experiments conducted on real-world dynamic datasets demonstrate that the proposed system achieves state-of-the-art performance in camera pose estimation and dense reconstruction in dynamic scenes.

</details>


### [115] [PAINT: Partner-Agnostic Intent-Aware Cooperative Transport with Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12852)
*Zhihao Cao,Tianxu An,Chenhao Li,Stelian Coros,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAINT是一个轻量级分层学习框架，用于腿部机器人协作运输，仅通过本体感知反馈推断伙伴意图，无需外部力传感器或负载跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作运输中机器人需要通过物理交互推断伙伴意图，同时在复杂环境中保持稳定的运动操作。传统方法难以捕获和建模交互信号，且依赖外部传感器。

Method: 采用分层学习框架：高层策略通过意图估计器和师生训练方案推断伙伴交互力矩；底层运动骨干确保鲁棒执行。解耦意图理解与地形鲁棒运动，仅需本体感知反馈。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界实验中，PAINT在不同地形、负载和伙伴条件下实现了顺从的协作运输。框架可扩展到去中心化多机器人运输，并能通过替换底层运动骨干在不同机器人平台上迁移。

Conclusion: 负载耦合交互中的本体感知信号为伙伴无关的意图感知协作运输提供了可扩展接口。PAINT框架实现了轻量级部署，无需外部传感器，具有良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Collaborative transport requires robots to infer partner intent through physical interaction while maintaining stable loco-manipulation. This becomes particularly challenging in complex environments, where interaction signals are difficult to capture and model. We present PAINT, a lightweight yet efficient hierarchical learning framework for partner-agonistic intent-aware collaborative legged transport that infers partner intent directly from proprioceptive feedback. PAINT decouples intent understanding from terrain-robust locomotion: A high-level policy infers the partner interaction wrench using an intent estimator and a teacher-student training scheme, while a low-level locomotion backbone ensures robust execution. This enables lightweight deployment without external force-torque sensing or payload tracking. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate compliant cooperative transport across diverse terrains, payloads, and partners. Furthermore, we show that PAINT naturally scales to decentralized multi-robot transport and transfers across robot embodiments by swapping the underlying locomotion backbone. Our results suggest that proprioceptive signals in payload-coupled interaction provide a scalable interface for partner-agnostic intent-aware collaborative transport.

</details>


### [116] [Evolving the Complete Muscle: Efficient Morphology-Control Co-design for Musculoskeletal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12855)
*Lidong Sun,Wentao Zhao,Ye Wang,Huaping Liu,Fuchun Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于肌肉骨骼机器人形态与控制协同设计的高效优化框架，通过同时演化肌肉强度、速度和刚度参数，并在低维谱流形上进行探索，显著提升了学习效率和运动稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肌肉骨骼机器人研究通常使用固定肌肉生理参数模型，这种静态物理设置无法适应复杂任务的多样化动态需求，从根本上限制了机器人的性能上限。需要开发能够同时优化多个生理属性的形态与控制协同设计方法。

Method: 提出了完整肌肉骨骼形态演化空间，同时演化肌肉强度、速度和刚度三个关键生理参数。为解决由此带来的指数级探索空间扩张问题，提出了谱设计演化（SDE）框架，通过结合双侧对称先验和主成分分析（PCA），将复杂肌肉参数投影到低维谱流形上，实现高效的形态探索。

Result: 在MyoSuite框架的四个任务（行走、楼梯、丘陵和崎岖地形）上进行评估，该方法相比固定形态和标准演化基线表现出更优的学习效率和运动稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过同时演化多个肌肉生理参数并在低维谱流形上进行高效探索，谱设计演化框架能够实现肌肉骨骼系统形态与控制的协同优化，显著提升机器人在多样化复杂任务中的性能表现。

Abstract: Musculoskeletal robots offer intrinsic compliance and flexibility, providing a promising paradigm for versatile locomotion. However, existing research typically relies on models with fixed muscle physiological parameters. This static physical setting fails to accommodate the diverse dynamic demands of complex tasks, inherently limiting the robot's performance upper bound. In this work, we focus on the morphology and control co-design of musculoskeletal systems. Unlike previous studies that optimize single physiological attributes such as stiffness, we introduce a Complete Musculoskeletal Morphological Evolution Space that simultaneously evolves muscle strength, velocity, and stiffness. To overcome the exponential expansion of the exploration space caused by this comprehensive evolution, we propose Spectral Design Evolution (SDE), a high-efficiency co-optimization framework. By integrating a bilateral symmetry prior with Principal Component Analysis (PCA), SDE projects complex muscle parameters onto a low-dimensional spectral manifold, enabling efficient morphological exploration. Evaluated on the MyoSuite framework across four tasks (Walk, Stair, Hilly, and Rough terrains), our method demonstrates superior learning efficiency and locomotion stability compared to fixed-morphology and standard evolutionary baselines.

</details>


### [117] [OVAL: Open-Vocabulary Augmented Memory Model for Lifelong Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12872)
*Jiahua Pei,Yi Liu,Guoping Pan,Yuanhao Jiang,Houde Liu,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OVAL是一个用于物体目标导航的终身开放词汇记忆框架，通过结构化记忆描述符和概率探索策略提升长期导航效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体目标导航方法在孤立单物体导航中表现良好，但在终身记忆表示方面存在局限性，限制了在长期连续目标导航中的有效性。需要解决记忆表示的适用性限制问题。

Method: 提出OVAL终身开放词汇记忆框架，引入记忆描述符进行结构化记忆管理，并提出基于概率的探索策略，采用多值边界评分提升终身探索效率。

Result: 大量实验证明了所提系统的效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OVAL框架能够高效精确地执行语义开放任务中的长期导航，解决了现有方法在终身记忆表示方面的局限性。

Abstract: Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav) refers to an agent navigating to an object in an unseen environment, which is an ability often required in the accomplishment of complex tasks. While existing methods demonstrate proficiency in isolated single object navigation, their limitations emerge in the restricted applicability of lifelong memory representations, which ultimately hinders effective navigation toward continual targets over extended periods. To address this problem, we propose OVAL, a novel lifelong open-vocabulary memory framework, which enables efficient and precise execution of long-term navigation in semantically open tasks. Within this framework, we introduce memory descriptors to facilitate structured management of the memory model. Additionally, we propose a novel probability-based exploration strategy, utilizing a multi-value frontier scoring to enhance lifelong exploration efficiency. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficiency and robustness of the proposed system.

</details>


### [118] [FastGrasp: Learning-based Whole-body Control method for Fast Dexterous Grasping with Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12879)
*Heng Tao,Yiming Zhong,Zemin Yang,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FastGrasp是一个基于学习的移动机器人快速抓取框架，通过两阶段强化学习策略整合抓取引导、全身控制和触觉反馈，解决高速运动下的冲击稳定、实时全身协调和跨对象泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在高速运动下的冲击稳定、实时全身协调以及跨不同对象和场景的泛化方面存在根本性挑战，受限于固定底座、简单夹爪或缓慢的触觉响应能力，而物流、制造和服务应用中的移动机器人需要快速抓取能力。

Method: 采用两阶段强化学习策略：第一阶段使用基于对象点云的条件变分自编码器生成多样化的抓取候选；第二阶段通过最优抓取选择引导移动底座、机械臂和手部的协调运动。触觉感知支持实时抓取调整以处理冲击效应和对象变化。

Result: 在仿真和真实场景中的广泛实验展示了卓越的抓取性能，通过有效的仿真到真实迁移实现了跨不同对象几何形状的鲁棒操作。

Conclusion: FastGrasp框架成功解决了移动机器人快速抓取的关键挑战，通过整合抓取引导、全身控制和触觉反馈，实现了高速运动下的稳定抓取和跨对象泛化能力。

Abstract: Fast grasping is critical for mobile robots in logistics, manufacturing, and service applications. Existing methods face fundamental challenges in impact stabilization under high-speed motion, real-time whole-body coordination, and generalization across diverse objects and scenarios, limited by fixed bases, simple grippers, or slow tactile response capabilities. We propose \textbf{FastGrasp}, a learning-based framework that integrates grasp guidance, whole-body control, and tactile feedback for mobile fast grasping. Our two-stage reinforcement learning strategy first generates diverse grasp candidates via conditional variational autoencoder conditioned on object point clouds, then executes coordinated movements of mobile base, arm, and hand guided by optimal grasp selection. Tactile sensing enables real-time grasp adjustments to handle impact effects and object variations. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior grasping performance in both simulation and real-world scenarios, achieving robust manipulation across diverse object geometries through effective sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [119] [Tree Learning: A Multi-Skill Continual Learning Framework for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12909)
*Yifei Yan,Linqi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Tree Learning框架，通过根-分支层次参数继承机制解决人形机器人多技能持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，支持周期性和非周期性运动，在Unity仿真中实现100%技能保留率和高奖励性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人强化学习从单任务向多技能范式演进时，如何高效扩展新技能同时避免灾难性遗忘成为关键挑战。现有方法要么依赖复杂的MoE模型拓扑调整，要么需要训练极大模型，难以轻量部署。

Method: 提出Tree Learning框架：1) 根-分支层次参数继承机制，通过参数重用为分支技能提供运动先验；2) 结合相位调制和插值的多模态前馈适应机制，支持周期性和非周期性运动；3) 任务级奖励塑形策略加速技能收敛。

Result: Unity仿真实验表明，与同时多任务训练相比，Tree Learning在各种代表性运动技能上获得更高奖励，同时保持100%技能保留率，支持无缝多技能切换和实时交互控制。在超级马里奥式交互场景和古典中国园林自主导航两个任务中验证了性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: Tree Learning框架通过参数继承机制从根本上防止灾难性遗忘，支持人形机器人高效的多技能持续学习，在保持轻量部署的同时实现高性能和良好泛化能力。

Abstract: As reinforcement learning for humanoid robots evolves from single-task to multi-skill paradigms, efficiently expanding new skills while avoiding catastrophic forgetting has become a key challenge in embodied intelligence. Existing approaches either rely on complex topology adjustments in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models or require training extremely large-scale models, making lightweight deployment difficult. To address this, we propose Tree Learning, a multi-skill continual learning framework for humanoid robots. The framework adopts a root-branch hierarchical parameter inheritance mechanism, providing motion priors for branch skills through parameter reuse to fundamentally prevent catastrophic forgetting. A multi-modal feedforward adaptation mechanism combining phase modulation and interpolation is designed to support both periodic and aperiodic motions. A task-level reward shaping strategy is also proposed to accelerate skill convergence. Unity-based simulation experiments show that, in contrast to simultaneous multi-task training, Tree Learning achieves higher rewards across various representative locomotion skills while maintaining a 100% skill retention rate, enabling seamless multi-skill switching and real-time interactive control. We further validate the performance and generalization capability of Tree Learning on two distinct Unity-simulated tasks: a Super Mario-inspired interactive scenario and autonomous navigation in a classical Chinese garden environment.

</details>


### [120] [E2E-Fly: An Integrated Training-to-Deployment System for End-to-End Quadrotor Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12916)
*Fangyu Sun,Fanxing Li,Linzuo Zhang,Yu Hu,Renbiao Jin,Shuyu Wu,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E2E-Fly：首个将可微分物理学习与训练、验证和真实世界部署系统统一的四旋翼端到端控制框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四旋翼从仿真到真实世界转移学习的挑战，包括视觉渲染效率低、物理建模不准确、传感器差异未建模以及缺乏集成可微分物理学习的统一平台。现有系统缺乏系统化的零样本转移流程，阻碍了可重复性和实际部署。

Method: 提出E2E-Fly集成框架，包含敏捷四旋翼平台和全栈训练、验证、部署工作流。训练框架采用支持可微分物理学习和强化学习的高性能仿真器，并针对常见四旋翼任务设计结构化奖励。引入两阶段验证策略（仿真到仿真转移和硬件在环测试），通过专用低级控制接口部署到两个物理四旋翼平台，采用全面的仿真到真实对齐方法，包括系统辨识、域随机化、延迟补偿和噪声建模。

Result: 成功训练了六个端到端控制任务并在真实世界中部署，验证了框架的有效性。这是首个系统地将可微分物理学习与四旋翼训练、验证和真实世界部署统一的工作。

Conclusion: E2E-Fly提供了一个完整的端到端解决方案，解决了四旋翼从仿真到真实世界转移的关键挑战，通过系统化的训练、验证和部署流程实现了可重复的零样本转移，为四旋翼控制任务的实际部署提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Training and transferring learning-based policies for quadrotors from simulation to reality remains challenging due to inefficient visual rendering, physical modeling inaccuracies, unmodeled sensor discrepancies, and the absence of a unified platform integrating differentiable physics learning into end-to-end training. While recent work has demonstrated various end-to-end quadrotor control tasks, few systems provide a systematic, zero-shot transfer pipeline, hindering reproducibility and real-world deployment. To bridge this gap, we introduce E2E-Fly, an integrated framework featuring an agile quadrotor platform coupled with a full-stack training, validation, and deployment workflow. The training framework incorporates a high-performance simulator with support for differentiable physics learning and reinforcement learning, alongside structured reward design tailored to common quadrotor tasks. We further introduce a two-stage validation strategy using sim-to-sim transfer and hardware-in-the-loop testing, and deploy policies onto two physical quadrotor platforms via a dedicated low-level control interface and a comprehensive sim-to-real alignment methodology, encompassing system identification, domain randomization, latency compensation, and noise modeling. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically unify differentiable physical learning with training, validation, and real-world deployment for quadrotors. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework for training six end-to-end control tasks and deploy them in the real world.

</details>


### [121] [DINO-Explorer: Active Underwater Discovery via Ego-Motion Compensated Semantic Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12933)
*Yuhan Jin,Nayari Marie Lessa,Mariela De Lucas Alvarez,Melvin Laux,Lucas Amparo Barbosa,Frank Kirchner,Rebecca Adam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DINO-Explorer：基于语义惊喜信号的自主水下车辆主动感知框架，通过动作条件预测器和自我运动补偿实现高效事件检测与带宽优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主水下车辆（AUVs）大多作为被动数据记录器运行，捕获大量视频供离线审查，经常错过具有高科学价值的瞬态事件。需要从被动感知转向主动感知，这需要一个因果、在线信号来突出重要现象，同时抑制机动引起的视觉变化。

Method: 提出DINO-Explorer框架：1）在冻结的DINOv3基础模型的潜在空间中操作；2）使用轻量级、动作条件循环预测器预测短期语义演化；3）采用传出副本启发模块，利用全局池化光流来消除自我引起的视觉变化而不抑制真实环境新颖性；4）在变体遥测约束下评估异步事件分诊任务。

Result: 1）在固定工作点，系统保留了78.8%的后发现人类审查共识事件，触发确认率为56.8%；2）自我运动条件抑制了45.5%相对于未补偿惊喜信号基线的假阳性；3）在回放侧帕累托消融研究中，DINO-Explorer在验证的峰值F1与遥测带宽前沿上稳健占优，在选定工作点将遥测带宽减少48.2%，同时保持62.2%的峰值F1分数。

Conclusion: DINO-Explorer提供了一个鲁棒、带宽高效的注意力机制，成功将数据传输集中在人类验证的新颖事件周围，为自主水下监测提供了有效的主动感知解决方案。

Abstract: Marine ecosystem degradation necessitates continuous, scientifically selective underwater monitoring. However, most autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) operate as passive data loggers, capturing exhaustive video for offline review and frequently missing transient events of high scientific value. Transitioning to active perception requires a causal, online signal that highlights significant phenomena while suppressing maneuver-induced visual changes. We propose DINO-Explorer, a novelty-aware perception framework driven by a continuous semantic surprise signal. Operating within the latent space of a frozen DINOv3 foundation model, it leverages a lightweight, action-conditioned recurrent predictor to anticipate short-horizon semantic evolution. An efference-copy-inspired module utilizes globally pooled optical flow to discount self-induced visual changes without suppressing genuine environmental novelty. We evaluate this signal on the downstream task of asynchronous event triage under variant telemetry constraints. Results demonstrate that DINO-Explorer provides a robust, bandwidth-efficient attention mechanism. At a fixed operating point, the system retains 78.8% of post-discovery human-reviewer consensus events with a 56.8% trigger confirmation rate, effectively surfacing mission-relevant phenomena. Crucially, ego-motion conditioning suppresses 45.5% of false positives relative to an uncompensated surprise signal baseline. In a replay-side Pareto ablation study, DINO-Explorer robustly dominates the validated peak F1 versus telemetry bandwidth frontier, reducing telemetry bandwidth by 48.2% at the selected operating point while maintaining a 62.2% peak F1 score, successfully concentrating data transmission around human-verified novelty events.

</details>


### [122] [RMGS-SLAM: Real-time Multi-sensor Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12942)
*Dongen Li,Yi Liu,Junqi Liu,Zewen Sun,Zefan Huang,Shuo Sun,Jiahui Liu,Chengran Yuan,Hongliang Guo,Francis E. H. Tay,Marcelo H. Ang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧密耦合的LiDAR-惯性-视觉3D高斯溅射SLAM框架，用于大规模真实场景的实时位姿估计和逼真建图，通过并行执行状态估计与高斯初始化、级联策略和基于高斯地图的闭环检测来平衡实时性、定位精度和渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-based SLAM方法在大规模真实环境中难以同时实现低延迟位姿估计、与传感器流同步的3D高斯重建以及长期全局一致性。需要开发一个能够平衡实时效率、定位精度和渲染质量的综合解决方案。

Method: 1) 并行执行状态估计和3D高斯基元初始化与全局高斯优化；2) 引入级联策略结合前馈预测与基于体素的主成分分析几何先验，提高高斯初始化质量和优化收敛速度；3) 在优化后的全局高斯地图上直接进行闭环检测，通过基于高斯GICP配准估计闭环约束，然后进行位姿图优化；4) 收集了具有硬件同步LiDAR-相机-IMU和真实轨迹的大规模室外SLAM序列数据集。

Result: 在公开数据集和自建数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在不同挑战性真实场景中实现了实时效率、定位精度和渲染质量之间的良好平衡。系统能够实现连续密集建图，并保持大规模场景的全局一致性。

Conclusion: 提出的紧密耦合LIV 3DGS-based SLAM框架成功解决了大规模真实环境中实时位姿估计和逼真建图的挑战，通过并行处理、级联策略和基于高斯地图的闭环检测，在保持实时性的同时提高了定位精度和渲染质量，为大规模场景的实时SLAM提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS)-based Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) in large-scale real-world environments remains challenging, as existing methods often struggle to jointly achieve low-latency pose estimation, 3D Gaussian reconstruction in step with incoming sensor streams, and long-term global consistency. In this paper, we present a tightly coupled LiDAR-Inertial-Visual (LIV) 3DGS-based SLAM framework for real-time pose estimation and photorealistic mapping in large-scale real-world scenes. The system executes state estimation and 3D Gaussian primitive initialization in parallel with global Gaussian optimization, thereby enabling continuous dense mapping. To improve Gaussian initialization quality and accelerate optimization convergence, we introduce a cascaded strategy that combines feed-forward predictions with voxel-based principal component analysis (voxel-PCA) geometric priors. To enhance global consistency in large scenes, we further perform loop closure directly on the optimized global Gaussian map by estimating loop constraints through Gaussian-based Generalized Iterative Closest Point (GICP) registration, followed by pose-graph optimization. In addition, we collected challenging large-scale looped outdoor SLAM sequences with hardware-synchronized LiDAR-camera-IMU and ground-truth trajectories to support realistic and comprehensive evaluation. Extensive experiments on both public datasets and our dataset demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a strong balance among real-time efficiency, localization accuracy, and rendering quality across diverse and challenging real-world scenes.

</details>


### [123] [XRZero-G0: Pushing the Frontier of Dexterous Robotic Manipulation with Interfaces, Quality and Ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13001)
*Junming Wang,Teng Pu,Wingmun Fung,Jindong Wang,Shanchang Wang,Yuan Deng,Shuyuan Wang,Ziwei Liu,Kunhao Pan,Ping Yang,Peng Zhai,Yuxin Liang,Xiaofan Li,Jiabi Sun,Renchao Xu,Xiaotian Tian,Pengfei Yan,Guoqiang Ye,Liang Li,Qian Wang,Ruyi Gan,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XRZero-G0系统通过硬件软件协同设计，结合虚拟现实界面和闭环数据收集流程，大幅降低机器人灵巧操作数据的获取成本，实现机器人数据与无机器人数据的有效混合，达到20倍成本降低的同时保持性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量、动作对齐的演示数据获取是扩展灵巧机器人操作基础模型的主要瓶颈。当前基于人类演示的无机器人系统（如UMI范式）存在硬件人机工程学不佳、开环工作流程以及缺乏系统数据混合策略等限制。

Method: 提出XRZero-G0硬件软件协同设计系统，包括：1）配备顶视摄像头和双专用夹持器的虚拟现实界面；2）针对非本体感知数据的闭环收集、检查、训练和评估流程；3）研究机器人数据与无机器人数据的经验缩放行为和最优混合比例。

Result: 系统达到85%的数据有效性率，建立了透明的质量控制机制。实验表明，少量真实机器人数据与大规模无机器人数据（如10:1比例）混合可实现与纯真实机器人数据集相当的性能，同时将获取成本降低20倍。构建了2000小时无机器人数据集，实现零样本跨本体迁移到目标物理机器人。

Conclusion: XRZero-G0提供了一种高度可扩展的通用现实世界操作方法，通过硬件软件协同设计和优化的数据混合策略，显著降低了高质量机器人操作数据的获取成本，同时保持了性能水平。

Abstract: The acquisition of high-quality, action-aligned demonstration data remains a fundamental bottleneck in scaling foundation models for dexterous robot manipulation. Although robot-free human demonstrations (e.g., the UMI paradigm) offer a scalable alternative to traditional teleoperation, current systems are constrained by sub-optimal hardware ergonomics, open-loop workflows, and a lack of systematic data-mixing strategies. To address these limitations, we present XRZero-G0, a hardware-software co-designed system for embodied data collection and policy learning. The system features an ergonomic, virtual reality interface equipped with a top-view camera and dual specialized grippers to directly improve collection efficiency. To ensure dataset reliability, we propose a closed-loop collection, inspection, training, and evaluation pipeline for non-proprioceptive data. This workflow achieves an 85% data validity rate and establishes a transparent mechanism for quality control. Furthermore, we investigate the empirical scaling behaviors and optimal mixing ratios of robot-free data. Extensive experiments indicate that combining a minimal volume of real-robot data with large-scale robot-free data (e.g., a 10:1 ratio) achieves performance comparable to exclusively real-robot datasets, while reducing acquisition costs by a factor of twenty. Utilizing XRZero-G0, we construct a 2,000-hour robot-free dataset that enables zero-shot cross-embodiment transfer to a target physical robot, demonstrating a highly scalable methodology for generalized real-world manipulation.Our project repository: https://github.com/X-Square-Robot/XRZero-G0

</details>


### [124] [Learning Versatile Humanoid Manipulation with Touch Dreaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13015)
*Yaru Niu,Zhenlong Fang,Binghong Chen,Shuai Zhou,Revanth Senthilkumaran,Hao Zhang,Bingqing Chen,Chen Qiu,H. Eric Tseng,Jonathan Francis,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HTD方法，结合多模态Transformer与触觉预测，实现人形机器人灵巧的接触式移动操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人形机器人在现实世界中移动操作面临的挑战：需要全身稳定性、灵巧手部控制、以及频繁接触变化下的感知能力

Method: 1) 开发基于强化学习的全身控制器；2) 建立结合VR遥操作和人体-人形运动映射的数据收集系统；3) 提出HTD模型，将触觉作为核心模态，通过行为克隆和"触觉梦想"（预测未来手部关节力和触觉潜在表示）进行训练

Result: 在五个接触丰富的任务中，HTD相比最强基线实现了90.9%的相对成功率提升；潜在空间触觉预测比原始触觉预测效果更好，带来30%的相对成功率增益

Conclusion: 结合稳健的全身执行、可扩展的人形数据收集和以触觉为中心的预测学习，能够实现现实世界中多功能、高灵巧性的人形机器人操作

Abstract: Humanoid robots promise general-purpose assistance, yet real-world humanoid loco-manipulation remains challenging because it requires whole-body stability, dexterous hands, and contact-aware perception under frequent contact changes. In this work, we study dexterous, contact-rich humanoid loco-manipulation. We first develop an RL-based whole-body controller that provides stable lower-body and torso execution during complex manipulation. Built on this controller, we develop a whole-body humanoid data collection system that combines VR-based teleoperation with human-to-humanoid motion mapping, enabling efficient collection of real-world demonstrations. We then propose Humanoid Transformer with Touch Dreaming (HTD), a multimodal encoder--decoder Transformer that models touch as a core modality alongside multi-view vision and proprioception. HTD is trained in a single stage with behavioral cloning augmented by touch dreaming: in addition to predicting action chunks, the policy predicts future hand-joint forces and future tactile latents, encouraging the shared Transformer trunk to learn contact-aware representations for dexterous interaction. Across five contact-rich tasks, Insert-T, Book Organization, Towel Folding, Cat Litter Scooping, and Tea Serving, HTD achieves a 90.9% relative improvement in average success rate over the stronger baseline. Ablation results further show that latent-space tactile prediction is more effective than raw tactile prediction, yielding a 30% relative gain in success rate. These results demonstrate that combining robust whole-body execution, scalable humanoid data collection, and predictive touch-centered learning enables versatile, high-dexterity humanoid manipulation in the real world. Project webpage: humanoid-touch-dream.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [The Non-Optimality of Scientific Knowledge: Path Dependence, Lock-In, and The Local Minimum Trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11828)
*Mohamed Mabrok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 科学发现轨迹是局部最优而非全局最优，受历史偶然性、认知路径依赖和制度锁定影响，类似机器学习中的梯度下降


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学被视为揭示自然真理的最可靠方法，但科学发现的轨迹很少被作为一个优化问题来研究。本文认为科学知识体系在特定历史时刻代表的是局部最优而非全局最优，科学框架、形式体系和范式受到历史偶然性、认知路径依赖和制度锁定的显著影响

Method: 将科学发现轨迹类比为机器学习中的梯度下降，提出科学沿着可处理性、经验可及性和制度回报的最陡局部梯度发展。通过数学、物理、化学、生物学、神经科学和统计方法学的详细案例研究来发展这一论点，识别认知、形式和制度三种相互关联的锁定机制

Result: 识别了科学发展的三种锁定机制：认知锁定（思维模式固化）、形式锁定（数学和概念框架固化）、制度锁定（学术机构和奖励体系固化）。这些机制导致科学可能绕过本质上更优越的自然描述

Conclusion: 认识到这些锁定机制是设计能够逃离局部最优的元科学策略的前提。提出了具体干预措施，并讨论了该论点对科学哲学的认识论意义

Abstract: Science is widely regarded as humanity's most reliable method for uncovering truths about the natural world. Yet the \emph{trajectory} of scientific discovery is rarely examined as an optimization problem in its own right. This paper argues that the body of scientific knowledge, at any given historical moment, represents a \emph{local optimum} rather than a global one--that the frameworks, formalisms, and paradigms through which we understand nature are substantially shaped by historical contingency, cognitive path dependence, and institutional lock-in. Drawing an analogy to gradient descent in machine learning, we propose that science follows the steepest local gradient of tractability, empirical accessibility, and institutional reward, and in doing so may bypass fundamentally superior descriptions of nature. We develop this thesis through detailed case studies spanning mathematics, physics, chemistry, biology, neuroscience, and statistical methodology. We identify three interlocking mechanisms of lock-in--cognitive, formal, and institutional--and argue that recognizing these mechanisms is a prerequisite for designing meta-scientific strategies capable of escaping local optima. We conclude by proposing concrete interventions and discussing the epistemological implications of our thesis for the philosophy of science.

</details>


### [126] [GoodPoint: Learning Constructive Scientific Paper Feedback from Author Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11924)
*Jimin Mun,Chani Jung,Xuhui Zhou,Hyunwoo Kim,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GoodPoint方法，通过微调和偏好优化提升LLM生成建设性反馈的能力，在ICLR论文数据集上验证了其有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM有潜力变革科学研究，但作者主张应将其用于增强研究者能力而非完全自动化研究。为此，研究建设性反馈生成任务，旨在产生有针对性的、可操作的反馈来帮助作者改进研究和展示

Method: 1) 构建GoodPoint-ICLR数据集（19K ICLR论文，包含作者回复标注的有效性和行动性维度）；2) 提出GoodPoint训练方法：基于有效且可操作的反馈进行微调，并在真实和合成的偏好对上使用偏好优化

Result: 在1.2K ICLR论文基准测试中，GoodPoint训练的Qwen3-8B相比基础模型将预测成功率提升83.7%，在相似规模LLM中达到反馈匹配的新SOTA，甚至在精确度上超过Gemini-3-flash。专家人工研究进一步验证了GoodPoint提供更高的实用价值

Conclusion: GoodPoint方法能有效提升LLM生成建设性反馈的质量，通过作者中心的有效性和行动性维度评估，证明了其在增强研究者能力方面的实用价值

Abstract: While LLMs hold significant potential to transform scientific research, we advocate for their use to augment and empower researchers rather than to automate research without human oversight. To this end, we study constructive feedback generation, the task of producing targeted, actionable feedback that helps authors improve both their research and its presentation. In this work, we operationalize the effectiveness of feedback along two author-centric axes-validity and author action. We first curate GoodPoint-ICLR, a dataset of 19K ICLR papers with reviewer feedback annotated along both dimensions using author responses. Building on this, we introduce GoodPoint, a training recipe that leverages success signals from author responses through fine-tuning on valid and actionable feedback, together with preference optimization on both real and synthetic preference pairs. Our evaluation on a benchmark of 1.2K ICLR papers shows that a GoodPoint-trained Qwen3-8B improves the predicted success rate by 83.7% over the base model and sets a new state-of-the-art among LLMs of similar size in feedback matching on a golden human feedback set, even surpassing Gemini-3-flash in precision. We further validate these findings through an expert human study, demonstrating that GoodPoint consistently delivers higher practical value as perceived by authors.

</details>


### [127] [Narrative-Driven Paper-to-Slide Generation via ArcDeck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11969)
*Tarik Can Ozden,Sachidanand VS,Furkan Horoz,Ozgur Kara,Junho Kim,James Matthew Rehg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ArcDeck：基于多智能体框架的论文转幻灯片生成系统，通过结构化叙事重构方法提升演示文稿的逻辑连贯性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接将原始文本总结为幻灯片，缺乏对源论文逻辑流程的显式建模，导致生成的演示文稿在叙事流和逻辑连贯性方面存在不足

Method: 1) 解析输入构建语篇树并建立全局承诺文档；2) 基于结构先验指导迭代式多智能体精炼过程；3) 专用智能体在渲染最终视觉布局前迭代批判和修订演示大纲

Result: 实验结果表明，显式语篇建模与角色特定智能体协调相结合，显著改善了生成演示文稿的叙事流和逻辑连贯性

Conclusion: ArcDeck框架通过结构化叙事重构方法，在论文转幻灯片生成任务中实现了更好的逻辑连贯性和叙事质量，同时提出的ArcBench基准为该领域提供了新的评估标准

Abstract: We introduce ArcDeck, a multi-agent framework that formulates paper-to-slide generation as a structured narrative reconstruction task. Unlike existing methods that directly summarize raw text into slides, ArcDeck explicitly models the source paper's logical flow. It first parses the input to construct a discourse tree and establish a global commitment document, ensuring the high-level intent is preserved. These structural priors then guide an iterative multi-agent refinement process, where specialized agents iteratively critique and revise the presentation outline before rendering the final visual layouts and designs. To evaluate our approach, we also introduce ArcBench, a newly curated benchmark of academic paper-slide pairs. Experimental results demonstrate that explicit discourse modeling, combined with role-specific agent coordination, significantly improves the narrative flow and logical coherence of the generated presentations.

</details>


### [128] [The Long-Horizon Task Mirage? Diagnosing Where and Why Agentic Systems Break](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11978)
*Xinyu Jessica Wang,Haoyue Bai,Yiyou Sun,Haorui Wang,Shuibai Zhang,Wenjie Hu,Mya Schroder,Bilge Mutlu,Dawn Song,Robert D Nowak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HORIZON是一个跨领域诊断基准，用于系统构建任务和分析基于LLM的智能体在长视野任务中的失败行为，评估了GPT-5变体和Claude模型等SOTA智能体，提出了基于轨迹的LLM-as-a-Judge管道进行可扩展的失败归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在短期和中期任务上表现良好，但在需要扩展、相互依赖动作序列的长视野任务中经常失败。这些长视野失败行为缺乏系统表征，阻碍了跨领域的诊断和比较。

Method: 引入HORIZON基准，系统构建任务并分析LLM智能体的长视野失败行为；评估多个模型家族的SOTA智能体（GPT-5变体和Claude模型），收集3100+轨迹；提出基于轨迹的LLM-as-a-Judge管道进行可扩展和可复现的失败归因，并通过人工标注验证。

Result: 在四个代表性智能体领域收集了3100+轨迹，研究了视野依赖的退化模式；验证了LLM-as-a-Judge管道与人工标注的一致性（标注者间κ=0.61；人-判断者κ=0.84）。

Conclusion: HORIZON为系统、跨领域分析长视野智能体失败提供了初步方法论步骤，并为构建更可靠的长视野智能体提供了实践指导。发布了项目网站和排行榜，欢迎社区贡献。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents perform strongly on short- and mid-horizon tasks, but often break down on long-horizon tasks that require extended, interdependent action sequences. Despite rapid progress in agentic systems, these long-horizon failures remain poorly characterized, hindering principled diagnosis and comparison across domains. To address this gap, we introduce HORIZON, an initial cross-domain diagnostic benchmark for systematically constructing tasks and analyzing long-horizon failure behaviors in LLM-based agents. Using HORIZON, we evaluate state-of-the-art (SOTA) agents from multiple model families (GPT-5 variants and Claude models), collecting 3100+ trajectories across four representative agentic domains to study horizon-dependent degradation patterns. We further propose a trajectory-grounded LLM-as-a-Judge pipeline for scalable and reproducible failure attribution, and validate it with human annotation on trajectories, achieving strong agreement (inter-annotator κ=0.61; human-judge κ=0.84). Our findings offer an initial methodological step toward systematic, cross-domain analysis of long-horizon agent failures and offer practical guidance for building more reliable long-horizon agents. We release our project website at \href{https://xwang2775.github.io/horizon-leaderboard/}{HORIZON Leaderboard} and welcome contributions from the community.

</details>


### [129] [When to Forget: A Memory Governance Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12007)
*Baris Simsek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Memory Worth（MW）作为评估记忆质量的轻量级操作指标，通过跟踪记忆与成功/失败结果的共现频率，为记忆管理提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统缺乏对记忆质量进行原则性管理的操作指标。写入时的重要性评分是静态的，动态管理系统使用LLM判断或结构启发式方法而非结果反馈，无法适应任务分布的变化。

Method: 提出Memory Worth（MW）：为每个记忆维护两个计数器，分别记录该记忆被检索时任务成功和失败的次数。在平稳检索机制和最小探索条件下，MW几乎必然收敛于条件成功概率p+(m) = Pr[y_t = +1 | m in M_t]。

Result: 在合成环境中，经过10,000轮后，MW与真实效用的Spearman秩相关系数达到ρ=0.89±0.02，而从不更新评估的系统为ρ=0.00。在真实文本检索实验中，过时记忆的MW值降至0.17，而专业记忆保持在0.77。

Conclusion: MW提供了一种轻量级、理论上有依据的记忆质量评估方法，仅需每个记忆单元两个标量计数器，可集成到现有记录检索和结果输出的架构中，为记忆管理提供实用操作信号。

Abstract: Agent memory systems accumulate experience but currently lack a principled operational metric for memory quality governance -- deciding which memories to trust, suppress, or deprecate as the agent's task distribution shifts. Write-time importance scores are static; dynamic management systems use LLM judgment or structural heuristics rather than outcome feedback. This paper proposes Memory Worth (MW): a two-counter per-memory signal that tracks how often a memory co-occurs with successful versus failed outcomes, providing a lightweight, theoretically grounded foundation for staleness detection, retrieval suppression, and deprecation decisions. We prove that MW converges almost surely to the conditional success probability p+(m) = Pr[y_t = +1 | m in M_t] -- the probability of task success given that memory m is retrieved -- under a stationary retrieval regime with a minimum exploration condition. Importantly, p+(m) is an associational quantity, not a causal one: it measures outcome co-occurrence rather than causal contribution. We argue this is still a useful operational signal for memory governance, and we validate it empirically in a controlled synthetic environment where ground-truth utility is known: after 10,000 episodes, the Spearman rank-correlation between Memory Worth and true utilities reaches rho = 0.89 +/- 0.02 across 20 independent seeds, compared to rho = 0.00 for systems that never update their assessments. A retrieval-realistic micro-experiment with real text and neural embedding retrieval (all-MiniLM-L6-v2) further shows stale memories crossing the low-value threshold (MW = 0.17) while specialist memories remain high-value (MW = 0.77) across 3,000 episodes. The estimator requires only two scalar counters per memory unit and can be added to architectures that already log retrievals and episode outcomes.

</details>


### [130] [Identity as Attractor: Geometric Evidence for Persistent Agent Architecture in LLM Activation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12016)
*Vladimir Vasilenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验证明大型语言模型中智能体身份文档（cognitive_core）在激活空间中表现出类似吸引子的几何特性，语义相关的表述会收敛到比结构匹配控制组更紧密的聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索大型语言模型中智能体身份文档是否表现出类似吸引子的行为，即语义相关的表述是否会映射到相似的内部表示，从而验证智能体身份在模型内部是否形成稳定的表征结构。

Method: 使用Llama 3.1 8B Instruct模型进行控制实验，比较三种条件：原始cognitive_core（条件A）、七个语义改写版本（条件B）和七个结构匹配控制组（条件C）。在8、16、24层进行平均池化状态分析，使用Cohen's d效应量和统计显著性检验。在Gemma 2 9B模型上进行跨架构验证，并进行消融实验分析语义和结构因素的影响。

Result: 语义改写的表述比结构匹配控制组收敛到更紧密的聚类（Cohen's d > 1.88, p < 10^{-27}, Bonferroni校正）。跨架构验证在Gemma 2 9B上得到确认。消融实验表明效应主要是语义驱动而非结构驱动，但结构完整性对于达到吸引子区域是必要的。探索性实验显示阅读智能体的科学描述会使内部状态向吸引子移动，区别于虚假预印本。

Conclusion: 智能体身份文档在LLM激活空间中诱导出类似吸引子的几何特性，语义相关的表述会收敛到相似的内部表示，这为智能体身份在语言模型中的表征提供了证据，并区分了"了解身份"和"作为身份运作"的不同认知状态。

Abstract: Large language models map semantically related prompts to similar internal representations -- a phenomenon interpretable as attractor-like dynamics. We ask whether the identity document of a persistent cognitive agent (its cognitive_core) exhibits analogous attractor-like behavior. We present a controlled experiment on Llama 3.1 8B Instruct, comparing hidden states of an original cognitive_core (Condition A), seven paraphrases (Condition B), and seven structurally matched controls (Condition C). Mean-pooled states at layers 8, 16, and 24 show that paraphrases converge to a tighter cluster than controls (Cohen's d > 1.88, p < 10^{-27}, Bonferroni-corrected). Replication on Gemma 2 9B confirms cross-architecture generalizability. Ablations suggest the effect is primarily semantic rather than structural, and that structural completeness appears necessary to reach the attractor region. An exploratory experiment shows that reading a scientific description of the agent shifts internal state toward the attractor -- closer than a sham preprint -- distinguishing knowing about an identity from operating as that identity. These results provide representational evidence that agent identity documents induce attractor-like geometry in LLM activation space.

</details>


### [131] [WiseOWL: A Methodology for Evaluating Ontological Descriptiveness and Semantic Correctness for Ontology Reuse and Ontology Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12025)
*Aryan Singh Dalal,Maria Baloch,Asiyah Yu Lin,Anna Maria Masci,Kathleen M. Jagodnik,Hande Kucuk McGinty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WiseOWL是一种帮助选择重用本体的方法论，通过四个指标（文档覆盖度、标签定义对齐、结构连接性、层次平衡性）进行评分并提供可操作反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义网标准化了概念含义，使机器可操作内容并促进高级分析。重用本体可加速开发并确保一致性，但选择最优本体具有挑战性，因为作者缺乏系统选择标准，通常依赖难以证明的直觉，限制了重用。

Method: 提出WiseOWL方法论，包含四个评分指标：1) Well-Described：衡量文档覆盖度；2) Well-Defined：使用最先进的嵌入技术评估标签与定义的对齐；3) Connection：捕捉结构互连性；4) Hierarchical Breadth：反映层次平衡性。方法输出0-10标准化分数并提供可操作反馈，实现为Streamlit应用，可输入OWL格式并转换为RDF Turtle，提供交互式可视化。

Result: 在六个本体上进行了评估，包括植物本体(PO)、基因本体(GO)、语义科学集成本体(SIO)、食物本体(FoodON)、都柏林核心(DC)和GoodRelations，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: WiseOWL为选择重用本体提供了系统化、可量化的方法论，解决了依赖直觉选择本体的局限性，促进了本体的有效重用。

Abstract: The Semantic Web standardizes concept meaning for humans and machines, enabling machine-operable content and consistent interpretation that improves advanced analytics. Reusing ontologies speeds development and enforces consistency, yet selecting the optimal choice is challenging because authors lack systematic selection criteria and often rely on intuition that is difficult to justify, limiting reuse. To solve this, WiseOWL is proposed, a methodology with scoring and guidance to select ontologies for reuse. It scores four metrics: (i) Well-Described, measuring documentation coverage; (ii) Well-Defined, using state-of-the-art embeddings to assess label-definition alignment; (iii) Connection, capturing structural interconnectedness; and (iv) Hierarchical Breadth, reflecting hierarchical balance. WiseOWL outputs normalized 0-10 scores with actionable feedback. Implemented as a Streamlit app, it ingests OWL format, converts to RDF Turtle, and provides interactive visualizations. Evaluation across six ontologies, including the Plant Ontology (PO), Gene Ontology (GO), Semanticscience Integrated Ontology (SIO), Food Ontology (FoodON), Dublin Core (DC), and GoodRelations, demonstrates promising effectiveness.

</details>


### [132] [Memory as Metabolism: A Design for Companion Knowledge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12034)
*Stefan Miteski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出针对个人LLM知识维基的伴侣式治理框架，解决用户耦合漂移导致的认知固化问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前检索增强生成模式主导LLM持久记忆，但个人维基式记忆架构（如Karpathy、MemPalace、LLM Wiki v2）缺乏针对单用户知识库中用户耦合漂移导致认知固化的治理机制

Method: 提出伴侣式治理框架，包含规范性义务、时间结构化程序规则和可测试一致性不变量。通过TRIAGE、DECAY、CONTEXTUALIZE、CONSOLIDATE、AUDIT五个操作实现，支持记忆重力和少数假设保留机制

Result: 核心预测：积累的矛盾证据应通过多周期缓冲压力积累的结构路径更新受中心性保护的主导解释，这是现有基准无法捕捉的失效模式

Conclusion: 个人LLM记忆是伴侣系统，应在操作维度上镜像用户，在认知失效模式上进行补偿。该框架在单智能体层面的安全性是部分的，明确界定了其解决和未解决的问题

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation remains the dominant pattern for giving LLMs persistent memory, but a visible cluster of personal wiki-style memory architectures emerged in April 2026 -- design proposals from Karpathy, MemPalace, and LLM Wiki v2 that compile knowledge into an interlinked artifact for long-term use by a single user. They sit alongside production memory systems that the major labs have shipped for over a year, and an active academic lineage including MemGPT, Generative Agents, Mem0, Zep, A-Mem, MemMachine, SleepGate, and Second Me. Within a 2026 landscape of emerging governance frameworks for agent context and memory -- including Context Cartography and MemOS -- this paper proposes a companion-specific governance profile: a set of normative obligations, a time-structured procedural rule, and testable conformance invariants for the specific failure mode of entrenchment under user-coupled drift in single-user knowledge wikis built on the LLM wiki pattern.
  The design principle is that personal LLM memory is a companion system: its job is to mirror the user on operational dimensions (working vocabulary, load-bearing structure, continuity of context) and compensate on epistemic failure modes (entrenchment, suppression of contradicting evidence, Kuhnian ossification). Five operations implement this split -- TRIAGE, DECAY, CONTEXTUALIZE, CONSOLIDATE, AUDIT -- supported by memory gravity and minority-hypothesis retention. The sharpest prediction: accumulated contradictory evidence should have a structural path to updating a centrality-protected dominant interpretation through multi-cycle buffer pressure accumulation, a failure mode no existing benchmark captures. The safety story at the single-agent level is partial, and the paper is explicit about what it does and does not solve.

</details>


### [133] [Mathematics Teachers Interactions with a Multi-Agent System for Personalized Problem Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12066)
*Candace Walkington,Theodora Beauchamp,Fareya Ikram,Merve Koçyiğit Gürbüz,Fangli Xia,Margan Lee,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究探讨了基于多智能体教师参与系统为中学生数学问题个性化定制的效果，发现师生主要关注真实性和适配性，而系统检测到的现实性问题在实际使用中较少出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用大语言模型和多智能体系统支持教师为中学生数学问题进行个性化定制，同时保持教师控制权，以提升学习效果。

Method: 开发了教师参与的多智能体系统：教师输入基础问题和主题，LLM生成问题，四个AI智能体分别从数学准确性、真实性、可读性和现实性四个专业维度评估问题。八名中学数学教师使用该系统在ASSISTments平台上创建了212个问题并分配给学生。

Result: 师生主要希望修改问题的真实世界情境的细粒度个性化元素，表明存在真实性和适配性问题。虽然智能体在问题编写过程中检测到许多现实性问题，但最终版本中师生反馈的现实性问题较少。可读性问题和数学幻觉也相对罕见。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统能够有效支持个性化问题生成，同时保持教师控制权。系统检测到的现实性问题与实际使用中的反馈存在差异，师生更关注真实性和情境适配。这为支持教师控制的个性化多智能体系统提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models can increasingly adapt educational tasks to learners characteristics. In the present study, we examine a multi-agent teacher-in-the-loop system for personalizing middle school math problems. The teacher enters a base problem and desired topic, the LLM generates the problem, and then four AI agents evaluate the problem using criteria that each specializes in (mathematical accuracy, authenticity, readability, and realism). Eight middle school mathematics teachers created 212 problems in ASSISTments using the system and assigned these problems to their students. We find that both teachers and students wanted to modify the fine-grained personalized elements of the real-world context of the problems, signaling issues with authenticity and fit. Although the agents detected many issues with realism as the problems were being written, there were few realism issues noted by teachers and students in the final versions. Issues with readability and mathematical hallucinations were also somewhat rare. Implications for multi-agent systems for personalization that support teacher control are given.

</details>


### [134] [Aethon: A Reference-Based Replication Primitive for Constant-Time Instantiation of Stateful AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12129)
*Swanand Rao,Kiran Kashalkar,Parvathi Somashekar,Priya Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Aethon提出基于引用的复制原语，实现近乎恒定时间的状态化AI智能体实例化，通过组合视图而非完全物化对象来降低延迟和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无状态模型推理到状态化智能体执行的转变正在重塑现代AI基础设施的系统假设。现有运行时架构受限于物化繁重的实例化模型，导致显著的延迟和内存开销，而大语言模型使得持久化、工具使用和协作智能体在技术上成为可能。

Method: 引入Aethon，一种基于引用的复制原语，将每个智能体实例表示为稳定定义、分层内存和本地上下文覆盖的组合视图。采用分层继承和写时复制语义，将实例化从复制转变为引用，使创建成本与继承结构解耦。

Result: Aethon实现了近乎恒定时间的智能体实例化，显著降低了延迟和内存开销。该系统支持复杂性、可扩展性、多智能体编排和企业治理，为生产级智能体软件提供了更合适的系统抽象。

Conclusion: 基于引用的实例化不仅是优化，更是生产级智能体软件的适当系统抽象。Aethon指向一类新的AI基础设施，其中智能体成为轻量级、可组合的执行身份，能够大规模生成、专门化和治理。

Abstract: The transition from stateless model inference to stateful agentic execution is reshaping the systems assumptions underlying modern AI infrastructure. While large language models have made persistent, tool-using, and collaborative agents technically viable, existing runtime architectures remain constrained by materialization-heavy instantiation models that impose significant latency and memory overhead.
  This paper introduces Aethon, a reference-based replication primitive for near-constant-time instantiation of stateful AI agents. Rather than reconstructing agents as fully materialized objects, Aethon represents each instance as a compositional view over stable definitions, layered memory, and local contextual overlays. By shifting instantiation from duplication to reference, Aethon decouples creation cost from inherited structure.
  We present the conceptual framework, system architecture, and memory model underlying Aethon, including layered inheritance and copy-on-write semantics. We analyze its implications for complexity, scalability, multi-agent orchestration, and enterprise governance. We argue that reference-based instantiation is not merely an optimization, but a more appropriate systems abstraction for production-scale agentic software.
  Aethon points toward a new class of AI infrastructure in which agents become lightweight, composable execution identities that can be spawned, specialized, and governed at scale.

</details>


### [135] [Towards Platonic Representation for Table Reasoning: A Foundation for Permutation-Invariant Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12133)
*Willy Carlos Tchuitcheu,Tan Lu,Ann Dooms*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文批判了传统表格表示学习中的序列化方法，提出了柏拉图表示假说，认为表格的语义稳健潜在空间必须具有内在的排列不变性，并开发了诊断指标和新型编码器架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格表示学习采用自然语言处理的序列化范式，这种线性化方法丢弃了表格的几何和关系结构，导致表示对布局排列变化脆弱。现有大型语言模型在表格嵌入中表现出严重的布局依赖偏差，使检索增强生成系统在表格检索时易受布局噪声影响而非语义内容影响。

Method: 1) 提出柏拉图表示假说，强调排列不变性对表格推理的重要性；2) 开发基于中心核对齐的诊断框架，包含两个指标：PI（测量完全结构打乱下的嵌入漂移）和rho（基于Spearman的指标，跟踪潜在结构向规范形式的收敛）；3) 设计新型结构感知表格表示学习编码器架构，明确实施单元格标题对齐的认知原则。

Result: 实证分析量化了现代大型语言模型的缺陷：即使微小的布局排列也会导致表格嵌入产生显著、不成比例的语义偏移。提出的新型编码器架构展现出优越的几何稳定性，并朝着排列不变性理想迈进。

Conclusion: 该研究对线性化表格编码器提供了基础性批判，并为语义稳定、排列不变的表格检索建立了理论框架，为信息系统中的表格推理指明了新方向。提出的结构感知编码器是实现排列不变性理想的重要进展。

Abstract: Historical approaches to Table Representation Learning (TRL) have largely adopted the sequential paradigms of Natural Language Processing (NLP). We argue that this linearization of tables discards their essential geometric and relational structure, creating representations that are brittle to layout permutations. This paper introduces the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) for tables, positing that a semantically robust latent space for table reasoning must be intrinsically Permutation Invariant (PI). To ground this hypothesis, we first conduct a retrospective analysis of table-reasoning tasks, highlighting the pervasive serialization bias that compromises structural integrity. We then propose a formal framework to diagnose this bias, introducing two principled metrics based on Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA): (i) PI, which measures embedding drift under complete structural derangement, and (ii) rho, a Spearman-based metric that tracks the convergence of latent structures toward a canonical form as structural information is incrementally restored. Our empirical analysis quantifies an expected flaw in modern Large Language Models (LLMs): even minor layout permutations induce significant, disproportionate semantic shifts in their table embeddings. This exposes a fundamental vulnerability in RAG systems, in which table retrieval becomes fragile to layout-dependent noise rather than to semantic content. In response, we present a novel, structure-aware TRL encoder architecture that explicitly enforces the cognitive principle of cell header alignment. This model demonstrates superior geometric stability and moves towards the PI ideal. Our work provides both a foundational critique of linearized table encoders and the theoretical scaffolding for semantically stable, permutation invariant retrieval, charting a new direction for table reasoning in information systems.

</details>


### [136] [Beyond Factual Grounding: The Case for Opinion-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12138)
*Aditya Agrawal,Alwarappan Nakkiran,Darshan Fofadiya,Alex Karlsson,Harsha Aduri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出当前RAG系统存在事实性偏见，过度关注客观事实而忽视主观意见，提出了一种基于不确定性理论框架的意见感知RAG架构，在电子商务卖家论坛数据上验证了其能显著提升检索多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统存在事实性偏见，过度偏向客观事实内容，将意见和多元视角视为噪声而非需要合成的信息。这种偏见限制了RAG在涉及主观内容（如社交媒体讨论、产品评论）的实际应用场景中的表现，并带来透明度和问责性风险：回音室效应放大主流观点、少数群体声音系统性代表性不足、以及通过偏见信息合成进行意见操纵的风险。

Method: 基于不确定性理论框架：事实查询涉及可通过证据减少的认识论不确定性，而意见查询涉及反映人类视角真实异质性的偶然不确定性。据此提出意见感知RAG架构，包含：1) LLM-based意见提取；2) 实体链接意见图；3) 意见增强文档索引。在电子商务卖家论坛数据上进行评估，比较意见增强知识库与传统基线。

Result: 实验结果显示在检索多样性方面有显著提升：情感多样性+26.8%，实体匹配率+42.7%，实体匹配文档上的作者人口统计覆盖率+31.6%。这表明将主观性作为一等公民处理能够产生可测量的更具代表性的检索结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为意见感知RAG提供了实证证据，将主观性作为一等公民处理能够产生更具代表性的检索结果。这是迈向意见感知RAG的第一步，未来工作包括检索和生成的联合优化以实现分布保真度。

Abstract: RAG systems have transformed how LLMs access external knowledge, but we find that current implementations exhibit a bias toward factual, objective content, as evidenced by existing benchmarks and datasets that prioritize objective retrieval. This factual bias - treating opinions and diverse perspectives as noise rather than information to be synthesized - limits RAG systems in real-world scenarios involving subjective content, from social media discussions to product reviews. Beyond technical limitations, this bias poses risks to transparent and accountable AI: echo chamber effects that amplify dominant viewpoints, systematic underrepresentation of minority voices, and potential opinion manipulation through biased information synthesis. We formalize this limitation through the lens of uncertainty: factual queries involve epistemic uncertainty reducible through evidence, while opinion queries involve aleatoric uncertainty reflecting genuine heterogeneity in human perspectives. This distinction implies that factual RAG should minimize posterior entropy, whereas opinion-aware RAG must preserve it. Building on this theoretical foundation, we present an Opinion-Aware RAG architecture featuring LLM-based opinion extraction, entity-linked opinion graphs, and opinion-enriched document indexing. We evaluate our approach on e-commerce seller forum data, comparing an Opinion-Enriched knowledge base against a traditional baseline. Experiments demonstrate substantial improvements in retrieval diversity: +26.8% sentiment diversity, +42.7% entity match rate, and +31.6% author demographic coverage on entity-matched documents. Our results provide empirical evidence that treating subjectivity as a first-class citizen yields measurably more representative retrieval-a first step toward opinion-aware RAG. Future work includes joint optimization of retrieval and generation for distributional fidelity.

</details>


### [137] [Development, Evaluation, and Deployment of a Multi-Agent System for Thoracic Tumor Board](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12161)
*Tim Ellis-Caleo,Timothy Keyes,Nerissa Ambers,Faraah Bekheet,Wen-wai Yim,Nikesh Kotecha,Nigam H. Shah,Joel Neal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发自动化AI图表总结工具用于肿瘤多学科会诊，替代手动流程，并验证LLM作为评估策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤多学科会诊需要简洁的患者病例总结以提高讨论效率和准确性，但现有手动AI工作流程劳动强度大，需要自动化解决方案

Method: 开发多种自动化AI图表总结方法，与医生金标准总结对比，使用基于事实的评分标准评估，部署最终状态自动化工具并进行后部署监测，验证LLM作为评估策略

Result: 报告了比较评估结果，成功部署了最终状态自动化AI图表总结工具，并验证了LLM作为基于事实评分的评估策略的有效性

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了将AI工作流程整合到常规临床实践中的成功案例，为肿瘤多学科会诊提供了高效的自动化总结工具

Abstract: Tumor boards are multidisciplinary conferences dedicated to producing actionable patient care recommendations with live review of primary radiology and pathology data. Succinct patient case summaries are needed to drive efficient and accurate case discussions. We developed a manual AI-based workflow to generate patient summaries to display live at the Stanford Thoracic Tumor board. To improve on this manually intensive process, we developed several automated AI chart summarization methods and evaluated them against physician gold standard summaries and fact-based scoring rubrics. We report these comparative evaluations as well as our deployment of the final state automated AI chart summarization tool along with post-deployment monitoring. We also validate the use of an LLM as a judge evaluation strategy for fact-based scoring. This work is an example of integrating AI-based workflows into routine clinical practice.

</details>


### [138] [Evaluating Relational Reasoning in LLMs with REL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12176)
*Lukas Fesser,Yasha Ektefaie,Ada Fang,Sham M. Kakade,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出关系复杂性（RC）作为评估大语言模型关系推理能力的新框架，发现模型性能随RC增加而单调下降，揭示了当前模型在高元数关系绑定方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型关系推理评估多关注结构化输入（如表格、图表）或合成任务，未能有效隔离高元数关系绑定带来的难度。需要一种原则性方法来控制混淆变量，专门研究关系绑定元数对推理能力的影响。

Method: 提出关系复杂性（RC）概念，定义为应用关系时需要同时绑定的独立实体或操作数的最小数量。基于RC构建REL基准框架，涵盖代数、化学和生物学领域，在每个领域内系统变化RC水平，同时控制输入大小、词汇和表示选择等混淆变量。

Result: 前沿大语言模型在REL基准上的表现随RC增加而一致且单调地下降，即使实体总数保持不变。这种失败模式在增加测试时计算资源和上下文学习后仍然存在，表明这是与所需关系绑定元数相关的固有局限性，而非推理步骤不足或示例缺乏所致。

Conclusion: 关系复杂性（RC）为评估大语言模型关系推理能力提供了原则性框架，揭示了当前模型在高元数关系绑定方面的系统性缺陷。研究结果强调需要重新审视现有基准，从关系复杂性角度分析模型的推理能力局限。

Abstract: Relational reasoning is the ability to infer relations that jointly bind multiple entities, attributes, or variables. This ability is central to scientific reasoning, but existing evaluations of relational reasoning in large language models often focus on structured inputs such as tables, graphs, or synthetic tasks, and do not isolate the difficulty introduced by higher-arity relational binding. We study this problem through the lens of Relational Complexity (RC), which we define as the minimum number of independent entities or operands that must be simultaneously bound to apply a relation. RC provides a principled way to vary reasoning difficulty while controlling for confounders such as input size, vocabulary, and representational choices. Building on RC, we introduce REL, a generative benchmark framework spanning algebra, chemistry, and biology that varies RC within each domain. Across frontier LLMs, performance degrades consistently and monotonically as RC increases, even when the total number of entities is held fixed. This failure mode persists with increased test-time compute and in-context learning, suggesting a limitation tied to the arity of the required relational binding rather than to insufficient inference steps or lack of exposure to examples. Our results identify a regime of higher-arity reasoning in which current models struggle, and motivate re-examining benchmarks through the lens of relational complexity.

</details>


### [139] [Beyond Scores: Diagnostic LLM Evaluation via Fine-Grained Abilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12191)
*Xu Zhang,Xudong Gong,Jiacheng Qin,Qiang Wang,JiaQi Liao,Zhe Wang,Dawei Feng,Bo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出认知诊断框架，使用多维项目反应理论评估大语言模型在数学等领域的细粒度能力，超越传统单一分数评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型评估将多样任务性能聚合为单一分数，掩盖了细粒度能力差异，限制了针对性模型改进和特定任务的能力导向选择

Method: 构建基于认知理论和领域知识的35维数学能力分类法，采用多维项目反应理论（IRT）和项目-能力关联矩阵估计细粒度能力水平，预测未见项目性能

Result: 在41个模型上验证，显示强标准效度、跨基准一致的能力估计，以及准确的未见项目预测（基准内AUC 0.80-0.89，跨基准AUC 0.77-0.86），显著超过基线；框架可推广到物理（27维）、化学（58维）和计算机科学（12维）

Conclusion: 建立了细粒度能力评估的原则性框架，在针对性训练、能力导向模型选择和能力感知基准设计方面具有应用潜力

Abstract: Current evaluations of large language models aggregate performance across diverse tasks into single scores. This obscures fine-grained ability variation, limiting targeted model improvement and ability-guided selection for specific tasks. Motivated by this gap, we propose a cognitive diagnostic framework that estimates model abilities across multiple fine-grained dimensions. For mathematics, we construct a 35-dimensional ability taxonomy grounded in cognitive theory and domain knowledge. The framework employs multidimensional Item Response Theory with an item-ability association matrix to estimate fine-grained ability levels, which in turn enable prediction of performance on unseen items (questions of benchmark). Evaluated on 41 models, our approach demonstrates strong criterion validity, consistent ability estimates across benchmarks, and accurate prediction of unseen items with AUC ranging from 0.80 to 0.89 within benchmarks and from 0.77 to 0.86 across benchmarks, substantially exceeding trivial baselines. The framework generalizes across scientific domains, producing consistent diagnostic performance in physics (27 dimensions), chemistry (58 dimensions), and computer science (12 dimensions). This work establishes a principled framework for fine-grained assessment of abilities, with potential applications in targeted training, ability-guided model selection, and ability-aware benchmark design.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Prompt: Fine-grained Simulation of Cognitively Impaired Standardized Patients via Stochastic Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12210)
*Weikang Zhang,Zimo Zhu,Zhichuan Yang,Chen Huang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StsPatient方法，通过提取对比指令-响应对的转向向量捕获领域特定特征，并引入随机令牌调制机制，实现对认知障碍患者的细粒度模拟，在临床真实性和严重程度可控性上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟标准化认知障碍患者为临床培训提供了可扩展且伦理的解决方案，但现有方法依赖离散提示工程，无法捕捉不同领域和严重程度水平的缺陷异质性。

Method: 提出StsPatient方法：1) 通过提取对比指令-响应对的转向向量创新性地捕获领域特定特征；2) 引入随机令牌调制机制调节干预概率，实现对障碍严重程度的精确控制，同时减轻传统向量方法的不稳定性。

Result: 综合实验表明，StsPatient在临床真实性和严重程度可控性方面显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: StsPatient方法能够实现对认知障碍患者的细粒度模拟，解决了现有方法在捕捉缺陷异质性和控制严重程度方面的局限性，为临床培训提供了更有效的模拟工具。

Abstract: Simulating Standardized Patients with cognitive impairment offers a scalable and ethical solution for clinical training. However, existing methods rely on discrete prompt engineering and fail to capture the heterogeneity of deficits across varying domains and severity levels. To address this limitation, we propose StsPatient for the fine-grained simulation of cognitively impaired patients. We innovatively capture domain-specific features by extracting steering vectors from contrastive pairs of instructions and responses. Furthermore, we introduce a Stochastic Token Modulation (STM) mechanism to regulate the intervention probability. STM enables precise control over impairment severity while mitigating the instability of conventional vector methods. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that StsPatient significantly outperforms baselines in both clinical authenticity and severity controllability.

</details>


### [141] [Designing Reliable LLM-Assisted Rubric Scoring for Constructed Responses: Evidence from Physics Exams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12227)
*Xiuxiu Tang,G. Alex Ambrose,Ying Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了使用GPT-4o对本科生物理手写建构反应进行AI辅助评分的可靠性，发现评分可靠性主要取决于清晰、结构化的评分标准，提示格式次之，温度设置影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STEM评估中的学生手写反应包含符号表达式、计算和图表，格式多样且解释困难，人工评分耗时且存在评分者间不一致问题。尽管大语言模型在AI辅助评分方面取得进展，但评分标准设计和LLM配置如何影响不同表现水平可靠性的证据仍然有限。

Method: 使用GPT-4o对20份真实手写考试反应进行AI辅助评分。四名教师和AI模型使用不同分析粒度的技能型评分标准进行两轮评分。系统变化提示格式和温度设置，比较人-AI一致性与人类评分者间可靠性。

Result: 人-AI在总分上的一致性接近人类评分者间可靠性，对高表现和低表现反应一致性最高，但对涉及部分或模糊推理的中等水平反应一致性下降。标准层面分析显示，明确定义的概念技能比扩展的程序性判断有更强的一致性。更细粒度的清单式评分标准比整体评分提高了评分一致性。

Conclusion: 可靠的AI辅助评分主要依赖于清晰、结构良好的评分标准，提示格式起次要作用，温度设置影响相对有限。研究为STEM环境中通过技能型评分标准和受控LLM设置实现可靠LLM辅助评分提供了可转移的设计建议。

Abstract: Student responses in STEM assessments are often handwritten and combine symbolic expressions, calculations, and diagrams, creating substantial variation in format and interpretation. Despite their importance for evaluating students' reasoning, such responses are time-consuming to score and prone to rater inconsistency, particularly when partial credit is required. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have increased attention to AI-assisted scoring, yet evidence remains limited regarding how rubric design and LLM configurations influence reliability across performance levels. This study examined the reliability of AI-assisted scoring of undergraduate physics constructed responses using GPT-4o. Twenty authentic handwritten exam responses were scored across two rounds by four instructors and by the AI model using skill-based rubrics with differing levels of analytic granularity. Prompting format and temperature settings were systematically varied. Overall, human-AI agreement on total scores was comparable to human inter-rater reliability and was highest for high- and low-performing responses, but declined for mid-level responses involving partial or ambiguous reasoning. Criterion-level analyses showed stronger alignment for clearly defined conceptual skills than for extended procedural judgments. A more fine-grained, checklist-based rubric improved consistency relative to holistic scoring. These findings indicate that reliable AI-assisted scoring depends primarily on clear, well-structured rubrics, while prompting format plays a secondary role and temperature has relatively limited impact. More broadly, the study provides transferable design recommendations for implementing reliable LLM-assisted scoring in STEM contexts through skill-based rubrics and controlled LLM settings.

</details>


### [142] [HintMR: Eliciting Stronger Mathematical Reasoning in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12229)
*Jawad Hossain,Xiangyu Guo,Jiawei Zhou,Chong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出提示辅助推理框架，通过分解数学问题为序列推理步骤并提供上下文感知提示，指导小型语言模型进行多步数学推理，形成协作双模型系统提升推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在复杂数学推理方面存在困难，主要因为其有限容量难以维持长链中间步骤且难以从早期错误中恢复，需要一种轻量级机制来增强其数学推理能力。

Method: 引入提示辅助推理框架，将解决方案分解为序列推理步骤，提供上下文感知提示。提示由单独的小型语言模型通过从强大大型语言模型蒸馏训练生成，与推理模型形成协作双模型系统。每个提示基于问题陈述和累积推理历史条件生成，提供逐步局部指导而不揭示完整解决方案。

Result: 在多样化数学基准测试和模型上的实验表明，提示辅助持续提升小型语言模型的推理准确性，相比标准提示方法获得显著增益，同时保持模型效率。

Conclusion: 小型语言模型之间通过提示生成和推理的结构化协作，为增强数学推理提供了有效且轻量级的机制，显著提升了复杂数学问题的解决能力。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) often struggle with complex mathematical reasoning due to limited capacity to maintain long chains of intermediate steps and to recover from early errors. We address this challenge by introducing a hint-assisted reasoning framework that incrementally guides SLMs through multi-step mathematical problem solving. Our approach decomposes solutions into sequential reasoning steps and provides context-aware hints, where hints are generated by a separate SLM trained via distillation from a strong large language model. While the hint-generating SLM alone is not capable of solving the problems, its collaboration with a reasoning SLM enables effective guidance, forming a cooperative two-model system for reasoning. Each hint is generated conditionally on the problem statement and the accumulated reasoning history, providing stepwise, localized guidance without revealing full solutions. This reduces error propagation and allows the reasoning model to focus on manageable subproblems. Experiments across diverse mathematical benchmarks and models demonstrate that hint assistance consistently improves reasoning accuracy for SLMs, yielding substantial gains over standard prompting while preserving model efficiency. These results highlight that structured collaboration between SLMs-via hint generation and reasoning-offers an effective and lightweight mechanism for enhancing mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [143] [MultiDocFusion: Hierarchical and Multimodal Chunking Pipeline for Enhanced RAG on Long Industrial Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12352)
*Joongmin Shin,Chanjun Park,Jeongbae Park,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MultiDocFusion：一种用于工业文档的多模态分块管道，通过视觉文档解析、OCR、LLM驱动的层次结构重建和DFS分组，显著提升RAG问答系统的检索精度和答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本分块方法在处理复杂工业文档时忽视文档结构，导致信息丢失和答案质量下降。工业文档通常具有复杂的层次结构（如章节、表格、图表），需要更智能的分块策略来保留结构信息。

Method: 1. 基于视觉的文档区域检测；2. OCR文本提取；3. 使用LLM进行文档章节层次解析（DSHP-LLM）重建层次树结构；4. 基于DFS的分组构建层次化分块。形成完整的多模态分块管道。

Result: 在工业基准测试中，MultiDocFusion相比基线方法：检索精度提升8-15%，ANLS问答分数提升2-3%。证明了显式利用文档层次结构对多模态文档问答的关键作用。

Conclusion: 结构感知的分块对于提升RAG问答系统的保真度至关重要。MultiDocFusion通过多模态方法有效捕捉工业文档的复杂结构，为文档处理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: RAG-based QA has emerged as a powerful method for processing long industrial documents. However, conventional text chunking approaches often neglect complex and long industrial document structures, causing information loss and reduced answer quality. To address this, we introduce MultiDocFusion, a multimodal chunking pipeline that integrates: (i) detection of document regions using vision-based document parsing, (ii) text extraction from these regions via OCR, (iii) reconstruction of document structure into a hierarchical tree using large language model (LLM)-based document section hierarchical parsing (DSHP-LLM), and (iv) construction of hierarchical chunks through DFS-based grouping. Extensive experiments across industrial benchmarks demonstrate that MultiDocFusion improves retrieval precision by 8-15% and ANLS QA scores by 2-3% compared to baselines, emphasizing the critical role of explicitly leveraging document hierarchy for multimodal document-based QA. These significant performance gains underscore the necessity of structure-aware chunking in enhancing the fidelity of RAG-based QA systems.

</details>


### [144] [ReflectCAP: Detailed Image Captioning with Reflective Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12357)
*Kyungmin Min,Minbeom Kim,Kang-il Lee,Seunghyun Yoon,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReflectCAP：通过多智能体分析LVLM的幻觉和遗漏模式，生成结构化反思笔记来指导图像描述生成，在事实性和覆盖度之间达到帕累托最优


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有详细图像描述方法难以同时实现事实准确性和细粒度覆盖度，需要在两者之间取得平衡

Method: 多智能体管道分析目标大型视觉语言模型(LVLM)的持续幻觉和系统性遗漏模式，将这些模式提炼为可重用的结构化反思笔记，在推理时用这些笔记指导描述模型

Result: 在8个LVLM模型上达到事实性和覆盖度的帕累托前沿，在CapArena-Auto基准上显著提升；相比模型扩展或现有多智能体管道，计算成本降低21-36%

Conclusion: ReflectCAP在事实性和覆盖度之间提供了更优的权衡，使高质量详细图像描述在现实世界的成本和延迟约束下变得可行

Abstract: Detailed image captioning demands both factual grounding and fine-grained coverage, yet existing methods have struggled to achieve them simultaneously. We address this tension with Reflective Note-Guided Captioning (ReflectCAP), where a multi-agent pipeline analyzes what the target large vision-language model (LVLM) consistently hallucinates and what it systematically overlooks, distilling these patterns into reusable guidelines called Structured Reflection Notes. At inference time, these notes steer the captioning model along both axes -- what to avoid and what to attend to -- yielding detailed captions that jointly improve factuality and coverage. Applying this method to 8 LVLMs spanning the GPT-4.1 family, Qwen series, and InternVL variants, ReflectCAP reaches the Pareto frontier of the trade-off between factuality and coverage, and delivers substantial gains on CapArena-Auto, where generated captions are judged head-to-head against strong reference models. Moreover, ReflectCAP offers a more favorable trade-off between caption quality and compute cost than model scaling or existing multi-agent pipelines, which incur 21--36\% greater overhead. This makes high-quality detailed captioning viable under real-world cost and latency constraints.

</details>


### [145] [Preventing Safety Drift in Large Language Models via Coupled Weight and Activation Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12384)
*Songping Peng,Zhiheng Zhang,Daojian Zeng,Lincheng Jiang,Xieping Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CWAC提出耦合权重和激活约束方法，通过同时约束权重更新和正则化安全关键特征，在微调中稳健保持LLM的安全对齐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法通常单独约束权重或激活，未考虑它们对安全性的耦合效应，导致LLM在微调期间安全对齐脆弱，即使良性适应也可能降解预训练的拒绝行为并产生有害响应

Method: 提出耦合权重和激活约束(CWAC)：1) 对权重更新施加预计算的安全子空间约束；2) 使用稀疏自编码器识别的安全关键特征进行针对性正则化；同时约束权重和激活以保持安全对齐

Result: 在四个广泛使用的LLM和多样下游任务上的实验表明，CWAC始终实现最低有害分数，对微调准确性影响最小，即使在高有害数据比例下也显著优于强基线方法

Conclusion: 同时约束权重和激活对于稳健保持LLM安全对齐至关重要，CWAC方法在保持微调性能的同时有效防止安全退化

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) remains highly fragile during fine-tuning, where even benign adaptation can degrade pre-trained refusal behaviors and enable harmful responses. Existing defenses typically constrain either weights or activations in isolation, without considering their coupled effects on safety. In this paper, we first theoretically demonstrate that constraining either weights or activations alone is insufficient for safety preservation. To robustly preserve safety alignment, we propose Coupled Weight and Activation Constraints (CWAC), a novel approach that simultaneously enforces a precomputed safety subspace on weight updates and applies targeted regularization to safety-critical features identified by sparse autoencoders. Extensive experiments across four widely used LLMs and diverse downstream tasks show that CWAC consistently achieves the lowest harmful scores with minimal impact on fine-tuning accuracy, substantially outperforming strong baselines even under high harmful data ratios.

</details>


### [146] [Heuristic Classification of Thoughts Prompting (HCoT): Integrating Expert System Heuristics for Structured Reasoning into Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12390)
*Lei Lin,Jizhao Zhu,Yong Liu,Donghong Sun,Hongbo He,Yihua Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HCoT方法解决LLM在复杂问题解决中的两个限制：随机生成导致决策轨迹缺乏确定性规划，以及推理与决策机制静态解耦导致知识无法动态调整推理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在解决复杂问题时存在两个主要缺陷：1) 推理过程呈现贝叶斯式随机生成，每个token从上下文依赖的概率分布中采样，导致决策轨迹随机而非确定性规划；2) 推理与决策机制静态解耦，动态检索的领域知识无法动态调整底层推理策略。这些缺陷导致初始决策缺乏战略锚定，推理链常无法收敛到正确解。

Method: 提出HCoT（启发式思维分类提示）方法，将LLM的推理能力与结构化问题空间协同。通过启发式分类模型控制推理过程并提供可重用的抽象解决方案，该方法集成到LLM生成过程中指导推理，兼容多种LLM且具有可重用性。

Result: 在两个复杂归纳推理任务中，HCoT在性能上优于现有方法（如Tree-of-Thoughts和Chain-of-Thoughts）。在结构良好的24点游戏任务中，HCoT相比最先进的Tree-of-Thoughts-BFS显著提高了token效率。在准确性和token使用方面，HCoT实现了帕累托前沿平衡，在性能和计算成本之间提供了良好权衡。

Conclusion: HCoT方法有效解决了LLM在复杂问题解决中的随机性和静态解耦问题，通过启发式分类模型引导推理过程，实现了性能与计算效率的良好平衡，为LLM的推理能力提供了结构化指导框架。
